<meta>catchword: the</meta>

to be, and not destroyed; this is proven from Holy Scripture, for **Christ**, many hundreds of years after the **Great Flood** <term>Sündfluß</term> <note>The biblical Deluge of Noah.</note>, said to the thief on the beam of the **Holy Cross** <note>original: "Stammen des heiligen Creutzes"</note>: "Today you will be with me in **Paradise**." This could not have been spoken of the heavenly **Paradise**, for **Christ** was the first man to enter **Heaven**, through whom **Heaven** was opened to his descendants. **Christ**, however, ascended toward **Heaven** only after many days; therefore, it could not be understood as the heavenly, but rather the earthly **Paradise**.

Secondly: **Christ** descended into **Hell** and redeemed the beloved **Patriarchs** <term>Altväter</term> <note>The righteous figures of the Old Testament.</note>; yet, as the Scripture says, he did not take them with him until his **Ascension** <term>Himmelfahrt</term>. *And he led captivity captive with him* <note>original Latin: "Et secum duxit captum captivitate captivos"</note>, and led the prison away captive. Where were they, then, during the time until the **Ascension**? Not in **Heaven**; nor in **Hell**, **Limbo** <note>original: "Vorhöll"</note>, or upon the Earth either: therefore, they must have waited in the earthly **Paradise** until the **Ascension**. 

From this, it is demonstrable that **Paradise** must still have existed at the time of **Christ’s** suffering. But if someone were to say now that it might well have been destroyed after the **Ascension**, so that it is no longer present today: to that I ask, where then are **Enoch** and **Elijah** now? They have not yet died, and yet they are not in the world, and nonetheless they are to return again to Earth

This is because nothing defiled can or will enter the **Kingdom of God** <note>original: "Reich GOttes"</note>, except for souls well purified through the **Blood of Christ**.

Since the body, however, must be purified through temporal death and through the **separation** <term>Separation</term> of the soul and spirit—as is commanded for every human body—it cannot be granted that they are in the heavenly [Paradise], but rather they must be in the **earthly Paradise** <note>original: "irrdische Paradeiß"</note> until the appointed time when they are meant to return. And because they are to return, they could not be in **Heaven**, for from the two extremes of **Heaven** and **Hell**, no one returns; but one may return from the **intermediate places** <note>original Latin: "Locis intermediis"</note>, that is, the **earthly Paradise** and the **infernal purgatory** <note>original Latin: "Purgatorio infernali"; here referring to a place of waiting or cleansing in the lower realms rather than the standard Catholic Purgatory.</note>. He says: "And **God** drove **Adam** out of **Paradise**, and left him on the Earth from which he came, so that he might till it."

# Secretum Magicum #

[God] took pity on the soul and first cursed the **serpent** <note>original: "Schlang"</note> as the instrument of the Fall, decreeing that it must crawl upon its belly and eat the dust of the earth. Through this process, their defiled bodies were intended to do penance and be purified once more.

But so that the soul might also be purified again, and the purified body then be reunited with the purified soul, God promised Adam that one should be born from the **seed of the woman** who would crush the head of the serpent—**Lucifer**, their deceiver—and redeem and purify their soul from him. Thus, the second person of the Godhead, the **Son of God**, the **Word of the Father**, became our mediator and the redeemer of our damned souls. ### Magical Secret ###

These are not literal stars, but the internal spiritual and planetary influences that govern the human body's functions and temperament.</note>, must destroy one another; thus the soul, which cannot dwell in a broken body, may separate itself again from the **Body of the Limbus**. In alchemy, decay is seen as a necessary stage of purification before a substance can be "resurrected" into a higher form.</note>. This matter shall afterwards, on the Last Day, be purified by the eternal fire and brought back into its first, fleshly, tangible, Christian, and eternal **Body** <note>original: "Corpus"</note>, such as the Son of God possessed according to his humanity after the resurrection. As Paul says: **A natural body is sown, a spiritual body shall rise** <note>original Latin: "Seminatur corpus animale, surget corpus spirituale." From 1 Corinthians 15:44.</note>. 

Then the purified, redeemed, and eternal soul will rejoice in its likewise purified, **glorified** <term>clarificirten</term> body, which has been restored to its original state. It shall reunite with the body and, being glorified through the contemplation of God, enter into the **New Heavenly Jerusalem** and rejoice with God for all eternity. ->### Of the three blessed magical [principles], etc.<-

S 5

...extract from one root the mercurial spirit of the Sun <note>original: "mercurialischen Geist Solis"</note> and the philosophical spirit. These are the two mercurial substances, and the single root is the **quintessence of the Sun** <note>original: "Quinta essentia Solis"; the fifth and purest element beyond the physical four.</note>, which is a father and the first beginning of all metals; for this reason, it is rightly called the **first root** <note>original: "Radix prima"</note>.

This body is twofold, yet it arises from a single origin. Reunite them by warming them subtly in their natural **water bath** <term>Water bath: In alchemy, a *balneum*, often used to mimic the gentle heat of a womb to foster "growth" in the vessel.</term>, so that they become one, just as they were before they emerged from the first root. 

And see to it that you do not hurry, and do not make the bath too hot for the King; otherwise, he might suffocate in it, and nothing would be born. Let him sit in this bath and sweat well until he is washed quite beautiful and clean and is well accustomed to the warmth. Then let him remain in it, with an orderly increase of the fire, until he has taken on a corporeal nature. At that point, the **King of Metals** <term>King of Metals: A common symbol for gold or the perfected Philosopher's Stone, representing the highest evolutionary state of matter.</term> is born, having taken on from the mother a metallic yet spiritual, pure body. This body is not "closed" <note>In alchemy, "closed" matter is stubborn and won't change; "open" matter is a "tincture" that can penetrate and transform other things.</note>, but rather "open." 

We would also possess such a body if we adhered to the Law and to prayer; for "daily bread" is the necessity for which we pray, and it causes no sickness. It is instead excess from which sickness and death follow.

# Magical Secret

<meta>catchword: sen</meta>

See now how merciful and kind **God** is: just as He has provided for the **Soul**—when it wanders from its nourishment in the Word of God and sins, whereby it becomes sick and mortally wounded—with certain good medicines, namely the **Sacrament of Wisdom** <note>original: "Sacrament der Weißheit"; likely referring to the Eucharist or divine spiritual insight.</note>, so that as often as the sinner sighs, his soul may be sanctified and obtain forgiveness of sins; so also God does not look so strictly upon the transgression and neglect of His own given **prescription** <note>original: "Recept"</note> and order concerning the body. 

He has mercy on the body, because it is the dwelling place <note>The body is viewed here as the 'house' or vessel for the soul.</note>, and creates for it the physician and the medicine out of the body’s mother—that is, from its mother, which is from **God**. This medicine now has the power and might to heal all diseases and to keep the natural body healthy in the balance of the elements until the appointed time; and if a certain term of life were not determined for man because of sin, or if death were not imposed as a punishment, it would be possible to maintain the body in eternal health. 

This, however, is not done through common, particular medicines, but rather the true **Universal** <term>Universal: Also called the Panacea, this alchemical substance was believed to cure all ailments by restoring the perfect balance of elements within the body, rather than just treating a specific ("particular") symptom.</term> must be present; for as proven before, man is a **Microcosm** <term>Microcosm: From the Greek for 'little world.' Alchemists believed the human body contained all the elements and forces of the 'Macrocosm' (the universe); thus, human health is inextricably linked to the laws of nature.</term>. Therefore, the whole great world must also sustain, nourish, and cure him. Now, if the **earth-fruit** <note>original: "Erd-Frucht"; referring to the physical body or the material result of the alchemical process in man.</note> of the Microcosm becomes sick and cannot help itself, then [the others] must...

This high medicine of the tincture, which lies locked within the body of gold, can rightly be compared to the Godhead, who is in Heaven and was barred from us humans after the Fall. **Vocabulary of Terms**
*   **Quintessence:** The purest, most concentrated essence of a substance.
*   **Microcosm:** The human being as a "little world" mirroring the universe.
*   **Temperament:** The healthy balance of internal qualities.
*   **Solis:** The Sun, used here to mean Gold.
*   **Tincture:** The transformative soul or essence of a metal or medicine.
*   **Medicine:** The universal healing agent or Philosopher's Stone.
*   **Gold:** The most perfect of metals, representing spiritual and physical purity.

### Secretum Magicum.

...helped, so that they all may be raised into the golden heaven, if they could turn to it; for the **Trinity of the Sun** <term>Trinitas Solis: the threefold nature of the solar principle, often corresponding to the alchemical trio of Sulfur, Mercury, and Salt</term> in the **Philosopher's Stone** <term>Philosophical Stone: the legendary substance capable of transmuting base metals into gold and acting as a universal medicine</term> can never leave that which it has taken on—namely, the **mercurial** soul-body <note>referring to Mercury, the principle of volatility and spirit</note>—but always comes to its aid. Just as it has been said of the **Stone of Gold**, in which the treasure of all nature lies, it is understood from the recounted Creation that a similar **magical stone** is to be found in the **limbo of the Microcosm** <term>limbo Microcosmi: the 'edge' or foundational substance of the human being, who is the "little world"</term>; for it contains all natures and **essences** <note>original: "Essentias"</note>, just like the golden stone; except for those differences already indicated in the Creation, which need not be repeated. Since every creature is brought back into its **First Matter** <term>Prima Materia: the original, undifferentiated substance from which all things are created</term> through death, it is therefore also possible to bring forth a new, nobler creature. And because the **Microcosm** <note>man</note> was created from all four **elements** in proper balance—even if it has been disordered by the **Curse** <note>the biblical Fall of Man</note> and rendered mortal—it follows that its recovered and truly dissolved matter also [reaches] into all four elemen-

-> ### Of three blessed magical, etc. <-

elements, must contain within itself an equal weight or virtue and their **essences** <note>original: "Essentias"</note>; and because every thing gives birth to its own kind, it follows that from this **Composite Limbo** <term>Limbo Composito: a Paracelsian term for the chaotic, primordial 'great matter' containing the seeds of all things</term> or **Matter** <note>original: "Materia"</note>, a new world is born. Observe now a herb-berry or roses: while they are still in their first life, they are indeed beautiful to the eye and pleasant in scent, but in their raw state, they can demonstrate only poor effects. However, if you take them and **dissolve** <note>original: "solvirest"</note> them—that is, destroy and kill them in their own nature, separating the pure from the impure through the **circulation** <note>a process of continuous distillation and condensation intended to refine a substance</note> of the elements, and join the elements together again to bring forth the new birth—you will then see how powerfully they manifest their effects in the human body, which they were not capable of in their first life.

Thus, as befits a **Physician** <note>original: "Medico"</note>, you must **prepare** <note>original: "præpariren"</note> all kinds of medicinal bodies and lead their **Tinctures** <term>Tincturas: concentrated spiritual extracts or medicines</term> and **Secrets** <term>Arcana: from 'arcanum,' the hidden healing power or 'mystery' within a natural substance</term> to the new birth, rather than using or administering the old or first form; and the **process** <note>original: "Processum"</note>

### The Magical Secret. Of three, etc.

...**process** <note>original: "Processum," appearing here as the fragment "ceffum"</note> in the preparation must be carefully observed, for **putrefaction** <term>Putrefaction: the essential alchemical stage of rotting or decomposition, where a substance "dies" to release its inner spiritual essence</term> is the most noble stage. In this stage, all living things rot and die, and all dead things therein are made alive; just as the little grain of wheat, mentioned by **Christ** himself, indicates to us: unless it is cast into the earth so that it may rot and die therein, it brings forth no fruit <note>A reference to the Gospel of John 12:24, frequently used by alchemists to explain that biological "death" is a prerequisite for spiritual or chemical growth</note>.
